# formatter meaning-preservation allowlist (ratchet ledger) — FormatterMeaningPreservationSweepTests.
#
# One row per failing cell bucket: `<stem> <twin> <bucket> # #<issue> <reason>`.
#   stem   — the fixture name (e.g. `basics/hello_world`)
#   twin   — identity (T0, the fixture itself) | comment (T1, `# cN` injected at every comment
#            anchor) | backtick (T2, every identifier backtick-escaped)
#   bucket — instrument | refused | unparseable | astChanged | commentDropped | escapeDropped |
#            notIdempotent
# A failing bucket that is not listed fails the suite, and a listed bucket that holds again fails
# too: delete the row when the cell is fixed (drain on fix).
#
# The contract (P22b, #2062 #2068 #2077 #2157): `sharpyc format` never rewrites bytes inside a
# string token and never drops a comment or an escape; Format(P) re-parses to an AST structurally
# equal to P's; the comment sequence and the escaped-name multiset are unchanged; Format is
# idempotent.
#
# Rules for adding a row:
#   1. It MUST cite a filed GitHub issue first: comment buckets #2077, escape buckets #2157 (the
#      class trackers); `instrument` rows cite #2077 (comment twin) or #2157 (backtick twin).
#   2. It MUST correspond to a real measured failure — never allowlist speculatively.
#
# Populated by the first run (P22b Phase 1); drained to EMPTY by the end of Phase 4.
# DO NOT DELETE THIS FILE: its presence arms the ratchet, and the sweep throws if it is missing.
